 # Last Animal — build LEDGER (updated 2026-10-04, MC 10059 record de-stale pass; prev. 2026-10-02 VM350 standby seat; M00 chain below: svarkor-session MC 1344)

Goal: Godot 4 C# ARPG 'Last Animal' — owner DoD (card 1344, verbatim): "spelbart spel utan buggar
som går att köra på en windowsdator" — a playable, bug-free game that runs on a Windows computer,
according to the specs (PHASE0.md: mandate I1-I6, constraints C1-C5, gate contracts C1-C17,
modules M00-M14).

Size note (file hygiene, MC 10059): one subject — this repo's project state — and fleet doctrine
names LEDGER.md the ONE ledger, so it is kept whole past the ~300-line split guideline.

CURRENT STATUS (2026-10-04): increments project — the "AAA goal" section below is authoritative
(REOPENED 2026-10-01, owner ruling quoted there). C16 Windows launch smoke remains the owner's
open acceptance step (never executed on Windows as of the 2026-10-04 workspace audit).

## AAA goal (owner ruling 2026-10-01, standing goal round 9; MC 3887 seat card)
STATUS: REOPENED 2026-10-01 as an increments project — owner ruling, verbatim: "Bryn1/last-animal is
the repo. The goal is a AAA game with graphics, story, gameplay, visualisera, skills, followers abd
more." Art style ruling: "Mix of both" (CC0 Kenney packs for environment/props + hand-composed
models for player/companion/boss/enemies). Scope ruling: "Everything." The M00 chain above remains
the historical base (MC 1344 achieved: playable, gates green, Windows-packageable).

Canonical remote: `git@github.com:bryn1/Last-animal.git`, master = pushed lineage
(df8ff23 base → graphics commits). Working branch `vm350/last-animal`. The prior remote on the
old (parallel-orchestrator) account is NOT this seat's to push; that account is now deleted (MC 10093).

ROOT CAUSE (VERIFIED by the new instrument at b107cd5, coords in evidence): terrain_builder.gd laid the HeightMapShape3D at the PNG PIXEL COUNTS (map_width=_w/map_depth=_d) — a HeightMapShape3D spans map_width x map_depth METRES (one sample per metre) while the visual mesh + navmesh span pixels x h_scale metres; h_scale is 1.5 meadow / 2.0 canyon / 1.6 ruins (meadow.tscn etc.), so EVERY shipped zone had a floorless ring beyond +/- map-half: meadow +/-32 m under a +/-48 m visible ground — main.tscn instantiates ONLY Meadow, the owner's exact fall; canyon +/-128 under +/-256; ruins +/-128 under +/-204.8. SECONDARY, VERIFIED: body.position y = v_scale*0.5 floated the remaining collider v_scale/2 above the surface (meadow hits median +0.82 m over a 0..1.5 ground — Godot 4.7 map_data is RAW local-Y, no top-origin pivot; the file's header claim "collision and rendering provably agree" was FALSE for h_scale != 1 — restamped). - MC 10759 / S47b CPU seam-fix pass over the five S47 plates (code child VM350, 2026-10-16, branch s47-textures off 4a6c381, commits 3ece659 (tool) + dc44e2c (plates+sidecars) + a71ddb8 (docs) + 6d755f4 (docs) + this restamp commit (docs + comment-only seam_fix wrap, MC 10759 ARCH-wall follow-up F-a/F-b), NO LEDGER row was ever filed for the MC 10523 GENERATION half — this row is the first S47 line here, disclosed): the five plates shipped FAILING the committed tileability gate on every one of the 12 draws (seam_max 118-224 vs interior_p95 20-54; attempts.json) and the S47 handback named three options; the PICK is the CPU wrap-fix, because 12 draws had already measured that prompt wording and seed do not move the metric (dirt series 118 -> 197 -> 234) and a true-tiling pipeline is out of this card. MECHANISM (`skeleton/ci/seam_fix.py`, NEW 103 l at 3ece659 / 105 l at this head (two comment-only line adds since), pure PIL + numpy = the seam_check.py deps, no GPU, no network, no new dependency): a tile is a torus, so the plate is blurred with PERIODIC support — its 3x3 period tiling through ONE isotropic gaussian, centre crop back — and every pixel is pulled toward that blur under a periodic raised-cosine weight w over the periodic distance to the wrap line (w=1 ON both wrap lines, w=0 at SEAM_BAND px inside, max of the x- and y-bells so the four edges and the corners are one pass). On a wrap line the crossing pixel pair then samples ONE smooth periodic field, so the seam gradient collapses to that field's own local gradient instead of the generator's edge step. sigma/band CHOSEN BY SWEEP not taste: 6/24 … 20/80 x 1-2 iterations measured on all five (both a two-pass per-axis and a one-blur union-weight form; the union form won — one blur, equal-or-better seam, less banding) -> sigma 10, band 40 is the smallest setting that keeps >=4.5x margin everywhere (ground_stone 6 vs p95 27 is the binding plate; ground_dirt hits 10x). GATE (skeleton/ci/seam_check.py, unchanged, before -> after seam_max/interior_p95, PASS verdicts): ground_dirt 118/32 -> 3/30, ground_moss 120/20 -> 3/19, ground_stone 172/28 -> 6/27, wall_stone 149/42 -> 3/41, wall_rock 224/54 -> 7/53 — FIVE OF FIVE PASS (restamp re-measure: python3 skeleton/ci/seam_check.py skeleton/resources/generated/*.png at this head prints the same five pairs PASS, exit 0), and interior_p95 moved by at most 2 units, so the pass is NOT bought by lowering the bar. PROPERTIES MEASURED, not assumed: max |diff| OUTSIDE the +/-40 px frame == 0 (byte-identical to the render, per plate); metadata chunks after the Image.frombytes re-encode tEXt/iTXt/zTXt/eXIf/iCCP/gAMA/sRGB == 0 on all five (the S47 strip property survives); re-applying the tool REFUSES (sidecar `postprocess` block, exit 1, SEAM_FIX_FORCE=1 documented as the override) so a double-apply cannot silently re-smooth a plate; PNG bytes SHRANK on all five (blend band compresses better). GATE RED-PROOF at THIS commit (law: a check that never failed is not a check): planted variant wall_rock_planted_bad.png = the FIXED plate with its left 256 columns rolled 64 rows (a genuine wrap mismatch, nothing to do with the generator) -> seam_check prints seam_max 158 / interior_p95 53 verdict FAIL (measured at the s47-textures tip this card ships, and re-printed identically — 158/53 FAIL — by the committed gate over the kept plant file at this restamp head; transcript out/red_proof/gate_proof_final.txt, which carries `git log -1` INSIDE the file so the sha binding lives in the evidence and not in a filename — this row first carried 213/51 written from the sweep, and the amend replaced the row with the transcript reading, never the transcript to fit the row), and the same run over the five shipped plates prints PASS 5/5 exit 0. P3 NAMED, not fixed (scope): seam_check.py exits 0 whatever the verdicts are — its contract is the printed verdict line, so a caller reading only $? would call five FAILs green; wiring an exit code is a change to the gate's own card, not something to smuggle in under a fix pass. Composites: out/fixed/ (green) + out/red_proof/ (red). PROVENANCE: each sidecar keeps prompt/seed/mode/quality/refs byte-unchanged as asset_finalize.py wrote them and gains ONE `postprocess` block (method sentence, sigma, band_px, tool, card, ts_utc, note naming what is NOT post-processed); LICENSES.md grew a Post-process column + the gate-status row now reads PASS with the numbers. CARRIED TO S41 (measured, honestly NOT fixed here — content, not seam): (1) wall_rock pale sky band, rows 0-63 mean luma 194-202 vs plate mean 74.2, 63728/65536 px above luma 150; the pass reaches only 185.3 / 60052 px (-5.8%) and the horizontal-wrap luma step 141.9 -> 124.1 — 77 rows (0-76) carry a majority of pixels above luma 150 — 77 px tall, wider than the 40-px seam band — so removal is a crop/repaint decision, and cropping them leaves 947 rows whose restore to Po2 1024 is an 8.1% vertical stretch: a composition call the integration gate owns, not a seam card; (2) generator-baked edge lighting seen as a low-frequency mean-luma step across the wrap (60-px strips, before -> after): ground_stone vertical +34.2 -> +29.9, wall_stone +22.8/+28.3 -> +19.7/+23.7, ground_dirt +10.3/+10.2 -> +9.1/+9.9 — the hard line is gone but tiling still shows that lighting swing (STYLE_BIBLE "no lighting gradient toward the edges" is a GENERATION miss); a low-frequency wrap-equalization (distributed per-row/per-column cosine ramp) would remove it but repaints the whole plate, so it is named for a ruling, not applied unilaterally. Visual QC: seam-crossing crops before/after compared by eye (out/ground_stone_seamcrop_BEFORE.png vs out/fixed/ground_stone_seamcrop.png) — slab/mortar edges continue across the boundary, no hard cut. No engine, no savegate, no push, no merge; the four other ci gates are untouched by this tree (measured at this head by git show --name-only over 3ece659^..HEAD inclusive: 1 Python file + 5 PNG + 5 JSON sidecars + 3 docs (LEDGER.md, LICENSES.md, docs/ARCHITECTURE.md) + .gitignore, nothing else — the restamp adds no new class).
